## Special Import Measures Act {#special-import-measures-act .Title-of-Act}

[R.S.C.]{.abbr title="Revised Statutes of Canada"}, 1985, c. S-15
:::

An Act respecting the imposition of anti-dumping and countervailing
duties
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-437281 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Special Import Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1984, c. 25, s. 1
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-437286 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this
  Act,

  [[amount of subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[amount of subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to any
      goods, means the amount determined in accordance with section
      30.4; ([montant de subvention]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[amount of the subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[amount of the subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1994, c.
      47, s. 144\]]{.Repealed}

  [[arbitration body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[arbitration body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the arbitration
      body referred to in Article 8.5 of the Subsidies
      Agreement; ([organe d'arbitrage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--United States--Mexico
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.65); ([Accord
      Canada--États-Unis--Mexique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canadian Secretary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian Secretary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when Part I.1 is in force, the Secretary
        referred to in section 14 of the [Canada--United States--Mexico
        Agreement Implementation Act](/eng/acts/C-10.65), and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when Part II is in force, the Secretary
        appointed under subsection 77.24(1); ([secrétaire
        canadien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2005, c. 38, s.
      132\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Committee on Subsidies
      and Countervailing Measures established by Article 24 of the
      Subsidies Agreement; ([Comité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, unless the context requires
      otherwise, includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an external or dependent territory of a country
        and any other territory prescribed by regulation made by the
        Governor in Council, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} except for the purposes of proceedings
        respecting the dumping of goods, a customs
        union; ([pays]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[country of export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[country of export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in the case of
      dumped goods, the country from which the goods were shipped
      directly to Canada or, if the goods have not been shipped directly
      to Canada, the country from which the goods would be shipped
      directly to Canada under normal conditions of trade and, in the
      case of subsidized goods, the country in which the subsidy
      originated; ([pays d'exportation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CUSMA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUSMA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country other than
      Canada that is a party to the Canada--United States--Mexico
      Agreement; ([pays ACEUM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Deputy Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Deputy Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1999, c.
      17, s. 180\]]{.Repealed}

  [[designated officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any officer, or
      any officer within a class of officers, designated pursuant to
      section 59 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6); ([agent
      désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[domestic industry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[domestic industry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, other than for
      the purposes of section 31 and subject to subsection (1.1), the
      domestic producers as a whole of the like goods or those domestic
      producers whose collective production of the like goods
      constitutes a major proportion of the total domestic production of
      the like goods except that, where a domestic producer is related
      to an exporter or importer of dumped or subsidized goods, or is an
      importer of such goods, [[domestic
      industry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} may be interpreted as meaning the
      rest of those domestic producers; ([branche de production
      nationale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dumped]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dumped]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to any goods, means
      that the normal value of the goods exceeds the export price
      thereof; ([sous-évalué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any duty, including provisional
      duty, imposed by virtue of this Act; ([droits]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[enterprise]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[enterprise]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a group of
      enterprises, an industry and a group of
      industries; ([entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[export price]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[export price]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means export price determined
      in accordance with sections 24 to 30; ([prix à
      l'exportation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[export subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[export subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a subsidy or portion
      of a subsidy that is contingent, in whole or in part, on export
      performance; ([subvention à l'exportation]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning
      assigned to the term [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} by
      section 2 of the [Canada-United States Free Trade Agreement
      Implementation Act](/eng/acts/C-10.6); ([Accord de
      libre-échange]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to any country
      other than Canada, means the government of that country and
      includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any provincial, state, municipal or other local
        or regional government in that country,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any person, agency or institution acting for,
        on behalf of, or under the authority of, or under the authority
        of any law passed by, the government of that country or that
        provincial, state, municipal or other local or regional
        government, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any association of sovereign states of which
        that country is a member; ([gouvernement]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[government of a CUSMA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[government of a CUSMA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      prescribed department, agency or other body of the government of a
      CUSMA country; ([gouvernement d'un pays ACEUM]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[government of a NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[government of a NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      2020, c. 1, s. 72\]]{.Repealed}

  [[importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to any goods, means
      the person who is in reality the importer of the
      goods; ([importateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means material injury to a domestic
      industry; ([dommage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[insignificant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[insignificant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a margin of dumping, a margin of
        dumping that is less than two per cent of the export price of
        the goods, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to an amount of subsidy, an amount
        of subsidy that is less than one per cent of the export price of
        the goods; ([minimale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[like goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[like goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to any other
      goods, means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} goods that are identical in all respects to the
        other goods, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the absence of any goods described in
        paragraph (a), goods the uses and other characteristics of which
        closely resemble those of the other goods; ([marchandises
        similaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[margin of dumping]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[margin of dumping]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to any
      goods, means, subject to sections 30.2 and 30.3, the amount by
      which the normal value of the goods exceeds the export price of
      the goods; ([marge de dumping]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[material injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[material injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1994, c.
      47, s. 144\]]{.Repealed}

  [[member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, R.S., 1985, c. 47 (4th
      Supp.), s. 52\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Public
      Safety and Emergency Preparedness; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s.
      72\]]{.Repealed}

  [[negligible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[negligible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of the volume
      of goods of a country, less than 3% of the total volume of goods
      that are released into Canada from all countries and that are of
      the same description as the goods. However, if the total volume of
      goods of three or more countries --- each of whose exports of
      goods into Canada is less than 3% of the total volume of goods
      that are released into Canada from all countries and that are of
      the same description --- is more than 7% of the total volume of
      goods that are released into Canada from all countries and that
      are of the same description, the volume of goods of any of those
      countries is not negligible; ([négligeable]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[non-actionable subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-actionable subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a subsidy that is not specific as determined
        pursuant to subsections (7.1) to (7.4),

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a subsidy for

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} industrial research assistance,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} pre-competitive development assistance,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} assistance to disadvantaged regions,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} assistance for the adaptation of existing
          facilities to new environmental standards, or

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} assistance for research activities conducted
          by institutions of higher education and independent research
          establishments,

        that meets the prescribed criteria, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (1.4), a domestic support
        measure for an agricultural product listed in Annex 1 of the
        Agreement on Agriculture, being part of Annex 1A to the WTO
        Agreement, that conforms to the provisions of Annex 2 to the
        Agreement on Agriculture; ([subventions ne donnant pas lieu à
        une action]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[normal value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[normal value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means normal value determined
      in accordance with sections 15 to 23 and 29 and 30; ([valeur
      normale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[North American Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[North American Free Trade
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s.
      72\]]{.Repealed}

  [[order or finding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[order or finding]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to the
      Tribunal,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} means an order or finding made by the Tribunal
        under section 43 or 44 that has not been rescinded under any of
        sections 76.01 to 76.1 and subsection 91(3) but, if the order or
        finding has been amended one or more times under any of section
        75.3, subsections 75.4(8) and 75.6(7) and sections 76.01 to
        76.1, as it was last amended, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} includes, for the purposes of sections 3 to 6
        and 76 to 76.1, an order or finding made by the Tribunal under
        subsection 91(3) that has not been rescinded under any of
        sections 76.01 to 76.1 but, if the order or finding has been
        amended one or more times under any of section 75.3, subsections
        75.4(8) and 75.6(7) and sections 76.01 to 76.1, as it was last
        amended; ([ordonnance ou conclusions]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a partnership and an
      association; ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a form, means
      prescribed by the President and, in any other case, means
      prescribed by regulation; ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[President]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[President]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the President of the
      Canada Border Services Agency appointed under subsection 7(1) of
      the [Canada Border Services Agency
      Act](/eng/acts/C-1.4); ([président]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prohibited subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prohibited subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a subsidy that is
      prohibited by virtue of being

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an export subsidy, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a subsidy or portion of a subsidy that is
        contingent, in whole or in part, on the use of goods that are
        produced or that originate in the country of
        export; ([subvention prohibée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[properly documented]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[properly documented]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a
      complaint respecting the dumping or subsidizing of goods, means
      that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the complaint

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} alleges that the goods have been or are being
          dumped or subsidized, specifies the goods and alleges that the
          dumping or subsidizing has caused injury or retardation or is
          threatening to cause injury,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} states in reasonable detail the facts on
          which the allegations referred to in subparagraph (i) are
          based, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} makes such other representations as the
          complainant deems relevant to the complaint, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the complainant provides

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the information that is available to the
          complainant to support the facts referred to in subparagraph
          (a)(ii),

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} such information as is prescribed, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} such other information as the President may
          reasonably require; ([dossier complet]{.DefinedTermLink
          lang="fr"})

  [[provisional duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provisional duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means duty imposed under
      section 8; ([droits provisoires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[regular member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[regular member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, R.S., 1985, c.
      47 (4th Supp.), s. 52\]]{.Repealed}

  [[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods, to authorize the removal
        of the goods from a customs office, sufferance warehouse, bonded
        warehouse or duty free shop for use in Canada, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods to which paragraph 32(2)(b)
        of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies, to receive the
        goods at the place of business of the importer, owner or
        consignee; ([dédouanement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[retardation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[retardation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means material retardation of
      the establishment of a domestic
      industry; ([retard]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes leasing and renting, an
      agreement to sell, lease or rent and an irrevocable
      tender; ([vente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[scope ruling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[scope ruling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a ruling made under
      subsection 66(1) as to whether certain goods are subject to an
      order of the Governor in Council imposing a countervailing duty
      made under section 7, an order or finding of the Tribunal or an
      undertaking in respect of which an investigation has been
      suspended under subparagraph 50(a)(iii); ([décision sur la
      portée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Secretary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Secretary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2014, c. 20, s.
      428\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Subsidies Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Subsidies Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Agreement on
      Subsidies and Countervailing Measures, being part of Annex 1A to
      the WTO Agreement; ([Accord sur les subventions]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Subsidies and Countervailing Duties Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Subsidies and Countervailing Duties
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s.
      144\]]{.Repealed}

  [[subsidized goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subsidized goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} goods in respect of the production,
        manufacture, growth, processing, purchase, distribution,
        transportation, sale, export or import of which a subsidy has
        been or will be paid, granted, authorized or otherwise provided,
        directly or indirectly, by the government of a country other
        than Canada, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} goods that are disposed of by the government of
        a country other than Canada for less than fair market value,

      and includes any goods in which, or in the production,
      manufacture, growth, processing or the like of which, goods
      described in paragraph (a) or (b) are incorporated, consumed, used
      or otherwise employed; ([marchandises
      subventionnées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a financial contribution by a government of a
        country other than Canada in any of the circumstances outlined
        in subsection (1.6) that confers a benefit to persons engaged in
        the production, manufacture, growth, processing, purchase,
        distribution, transportation, sale, export or import of goods,
        but does not include the amount of any duty or internal tax
        imposed by the government of the country of origin or country of
        export on

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} goods that, because of their exportation from
          the country of export or country of origin, have been exempted
          or have been or will be relieved by means of remission, refund
          or drawback,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} energy, fuel, oil and catalysts that are
          used or consumed in the production of exported goods and that
          have been exempted or have been or will be relieved by means
          of remission, refund or drawback, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} goods incorporated into exported goods and
          that have been exempted or have been or will be relieved by
          means of remission, refund or drawback, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any form of income or price support within the
        meaning of Article XVI of the General Agreement on Tariffs and
        Trade, 1994, being part of Annex 1A to the WTO Agreement, that
        confers a benefit; ([subvention]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian International
      Trade Tribunal established by subsection 3(1) of the [Canadian
      International Trade Tribunal
      Act](/eng/acts/C-18.3); ([Tribunal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[undertaking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[undertaking]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[undertakings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an undertaking or
      undertakings with respect to goods that are the subject of a
      dumping or subsidizing investigation under this Act given in
      writing to the President in any of the following circumstances:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dumped goods, an undertaking
        given by an exporter who accounts for, or undertakings given
        individually by exporters who account for, all or substantially
        all the exports to Canada of the dumped goods where the exporter
        or each exporter, as the case may be, undertakes in his
        undertaking

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to revise, in the manner specified in his
          undertaking, the price at which he sells the goods to
          importers in Canada, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to cease dumping the goods in Canada, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of subsidized goods,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an undertaking given by an exporter who
          accounts for, or undertakings given individually by exporters
          who account for, all or substantially all the exports to
          Canada of the subsidized goods, where the exporter or each
          exporter, as the case may be,

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} has the consent of the government of the
            country of export of the goods to give the undertaking, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} undertakes to revise, in the manner
            specified in his undertaking, the price at which he sells
            the goods to importers in Canada, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an undertaking given by the government of a
          country that accounts for, or undertakings given by the
          governments of countries that account for, all or
          substantially all the exports to Canada of the subsidized
          goods where the country or each country, as the case may be,
          undertakes in its undertaking

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} to eliminate the subsidy on goods exported
            to Canada from that country,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} to limit the amount of subsidy on goods
            exported to Canada from that country,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} to limit the quantity of the goods to be
            exported to Canada from that country, or

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} otherwise to eliminate the effect of the
            subsidizing on the production in Canada of like goods,

          in the manner specified in its
          undertaking; ([engagement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
          [engagements]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[United States government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[United States government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means such
      department, agency or other body of the federal government of the
      United States as is prescribed; ([gouvernement des
      États-Unis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[WTO Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[WTO Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned to
      the term [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} by subsection 2(1) of
      the [World Trade Organization Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/W-11.8). ([Accord sur l'OMC]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When domestic industry based on
  regional markets

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} In exceptional circumstances, the territory of
  Canada may, for the production of any goods, be divided into two or
  more regional markets and the domestic producers of like goods in any
  of those markets may be considered to be a separate domestic industry
  where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the producers in the market sell all or almost all
    of their production of like goods in the market; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the demand in the market is not to any substantial
    degree supplied by producers of like goods located elsewhere in
    Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Producers related to exporters or
  importers

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definition [[domestic
  industry]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1), a domestic
  producer is related to an exporter or an importer of dumped or
  subsidized goods where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the producer either directly or indirectly
    controls, or is controlled by, the exporter or importer,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the producer and the exporter or the importer, as
    the case may be, are directly or indirectly controlled by a third
    person, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the producer and the exporter or the importer, as
    the case may be, directly or indirectly control a third person,

  and there are grounds to believe that the producer behaves differently
  towards the exporter or importer than does a non-related producer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where there is deemed to be control

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1.2), a person is
  deemed to control another person where the first person is legally or
  operationally in a position to exercise restraint or direction over
  the other person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When domestic support measure ceases to
  be a non-actionable subsidy

  [(1.4)]{.lawlabel} A domestic support measure referred to in
  paragraph (c) of the definition [[non-actionable
  subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1) ceases to be a
  non-actionable subsidy on the day on which the implementation period
  in respect of the Agreement on Agriculture referred to in that
  paragraph, as defined in Article 1 of that Agreement for the purposes
  of Article 13 of that Agreement, expires.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat of injury

  [(1.5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the dumping or
  subsidizing of goods shall not be found to be threatening to cause
  injury or to cause a threat of injury unless the circumstances in
  which the dumping or subsidizing of goods would cause injury are
  clearly foreseen and imminent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Financial contribution

  [(1.6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (a) of the definition
  [[subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1), there is a
  financial contribution by a government of a country other than Canada
  where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} practices of the government involve the direct
    transfer of funds or liabilities or the contingent transfer of funds
    or liabilities;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} amounts that would otherwise be owing and due to
    the government are exempted or deducted or amounts that are owing
    and due to the government are forgiven or not collected;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the government provides goods or services, other
    than general governmental infrastructure, or purchases goods; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the government permits or directs a
    non-governmental body to do any thing referred to in any of
    paragraphs (a) to (c) where the right or obligation to do the thing
    is normally vested in the government and the manner in which the
    non-governmental body does the thing does not differ in a meaningful
    way from the manner in which the government would do it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[associated
  persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the following persons
  are [[associated persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or persons associated
  with each other, namely,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} persons related to each other; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} persons not related to each other, but not dealing
    with each other at arm's length.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons related to each other

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), persons are
  related to each other if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are individuals connected by blood
    relationship, marriage, common-law partnership or adoption within
    the meaning of subsection 251(6) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one is an officer or director of the other;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each such person is an officer or director of the
    same two corporations, associations, partnerships or other
    organizations;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} they are partners;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} one is the employer of the other;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} they directly or indirectly control or are
    controlled by the same person;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} one directly or indirectly controls or is
    controlled by the other;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any other person directly or indirectly owns, holds
    or controls five per cent or more of the outstanding voting stock or
    shares of each such person; or

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} one directly or indirectly owns, holds or controls
    five per cent or more of the outstanding voting stock or shares of
    the other.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons dealing at arm's length

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(b), it is a
  question of fact whether persons not related to each other were at a
  particular time dealing with each other at arm's length.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 144\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement affecting countervailing duty

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the definition [[amount of
  subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, where, in relation to any subsidized
  goods, the manufacturer, producer, vendor or exporter thereof or the
  government of a country other than Canada, undertakes, directly or
  indirectly in any manner whatever, to indemnify, pay on behalf of or
  reimburse the importer or purchaser in Canada of the goods for all or
  any part of the countervailing duty that may be levied on the goods,
  the amount of subsidy on the goods is, for the purposes of this Act,
  the amount of subsidy determined and adjusted in such manner as is
  provided under that definition plus the amount of the indemnity,
  payment or reimbursement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation of provisions that apply
  to both dumped and subsidized goods

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Where, by its terms, any provision of this Act
  applies to both dumped and subsidized goods, the application of the
  provision

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to subsidized goods shall not be taken into account
    in an investigation, inquiry or other proceeding or matter under
    this Act relating to the dumping of goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to dumped goods shall not be taken into account in
    an investigation, inquiry or other proceeding or matter under this
    Act relating to the subsidizing of goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria and conditions for
  non-specificity

  [(7.1)]{.lawlabel} A subsidy is not specific where the criteria or
  conditions governing eligibility for, and the amount of, the subsidy
  are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} objective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} set out in a legislative, regulatory or
    administrative instrument or other public document; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} applied in a manner that does not favour or is not
    limited to a particular enterprise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When subsidy is specific

  [(7.2)]{.lawlabel} A subsidy is specific where it is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} limited, pursuant to an instrument or document
    referred to in paragraph (7.1)(b), to a particular enterprise within
    the jurisdiction of the authority that is granting the subsidy; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prohibited subsidy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of specificity by
  President

  [(7.3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding that a subsidy is not limited in
  the manner referred to in paragraph (7.2)(a), the President may
  determine the subsidy to be specific having regard as to whether

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there is exclusive use of the subsidy by a limited
    number of enterprises;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is predominant use of the subsidy by a
    particular enterprise;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} disproportionately large amounts of the subsidy are
    granted to a limited number of enterprises; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which discretion is exercised by the
    granting authority indicates that the subsidy is not generally
    available.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional considerations

  [(7.4)]{.lawlabel} Where any of the factors listed in paragraphs
  (7.3)(a) to (d) is present, the President shall consider whether the
  presence is due to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the extent of diversification of economic
    activities within the jurisdiction of the granting authority, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the length of time that the subsidy program has
    been in operation,

  and where the President is of the opinion that the presence is due to
  one of the reasons set out in paragraph (a) or (b), the President may
  find the subsidy not to be specific notwithstanding that, were it not
  for that opinion, the President would have found the subsidy to be
  specific.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Law relating to the customs

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, this Act shall be considered,
  for the purposes of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), to be a law
  relating to the customs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers, duties and functions of
  President

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Any power, duty or function of the President under
  this Act may be exercised or performed by any person authorized by the
  President to do so and, if so exercised or performed, is deemed to
  have been exercised or performed by the President.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} The [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies, with
  any modifications that the circumstances require, in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the payment, collection or refund of any duty
    levied or returned under this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the payment, collection, refund or waiver of
    interest on duty payable or returned under this Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the time within which duties payable under this
    Act, or security posted under this Act, are deemed to be paid or
    posted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assessment of injury --- impacts on
  workers

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} In any assessment of injury under this Act, any
  impacts on workers employed in the domestic industry shall be taken
  into account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assessment of retardation --- impacts
  on jobs

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} In any assessment of retardation under this Act, any
  impacts on jobs shall be taken into account.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 2
- R.S., 1985, c. 23 (1st Supp.), s. 1, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), ss. 197,
  213, c. 47 (4th Supp.), s. 52
- 1988, c. 65, s. 23
- 1993, c. 44, s. 201
- 1994, c. 13, s. 7, c. 47, ss. 144, 185
- 1999, c. 12, s. 1, c. 17, ss. 180, 183
- 2000, c. 12, s. 291
- 2001, c. 25, s. 91
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 132, 134, 135(E), 145
- 2010, c. 12, s. 1782
- 2014, c. 20, s. 428
- 2016, c. 7, s. 192
- 2017, c. 20, s. 68

  role="button"}

  role="button"}

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Special Import Measures]{.HTitleText1} {#h-437475 .Part}

### [Liability for Anti-dumping, Countervailing and Provisional Duties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-437476 .Subheading}

#### [Anti-dumping and Countervailing Duty]{.HTitleText3} {#h-437477 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[massive
importation]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** In this Part, [[massive
importation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a series of importations into
Canada that in the aggregate are massive and have occurred within a
relatively short period of time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 192](#1357058-1367825){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1357058-1367825.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Anti-dumping and countervailing duty

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to section 7.1, there shall be levied, collected and paid on all
  dumped and subsidized goods imported into Canada in respect of which
  the Tribunal has made an order or finding, before the release of the
  goods, that the dumping or subsidizing of goods of the same
  description has caused injury or retardation, is threatening to cause
  injury or would have caused injury or retardation except for the fact
  that provisional duty was applied in respect of the goods, a duty as
  follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dumped goods, an anti-dumping duty
    in an amount equal to the margin of dumping of the imported goods;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of subsidized goods, a countervailing
    duty in an amount equal to the amount of subsidy on the imported
    goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties --- circumvention

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A duty shall be levied, collected and paid on all
  dumped and subsidized goods imported into Canada in respect of which
  the Tribunal has made an order --- amending an order or finding,
  before the release of the goods --- to the effect that the importation
  of goods of the same description constitutes circumvention, as
  follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dumped goods, an anti-dumping duty
    in an amount that is equal to the margin of dumping of the imported
    goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of subsidized goods, a countervailing
    duty in an amount that is equal to the amount of subsidy on the
    imported goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties --- circumvention investigation

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} A duty shall be levied, collected and paid on all
  dumped and subsidized goods imported into Canada, after the initiation
  of an anti-circumvention investigation under section 72, in respect of
  which the Tribunal has made an order --- amending an order or finding
  after the release of the goods --- to the effect that the importation
  of goods of the same description constitutes circumvention, as
  follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dumped goods, an anti-dumping duty
    in an amount that is equal to the margin of dumping of the imported
    goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of subsidized goods, a countervailing
    duty in an amount that is equal to the amount of subsidy on the
    imported goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty where undertaking violated

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Tribunal has made an order or finding referred
  to in subsection (1) in respect of goods that are subject to an
  undertaking referred to in section 7.1 and the undertaking is
  subsequently terminated under paragraph 52(1)(d), there shall be
  levied, collected and paid a duty as provided under paragraphs (1)(a)
  and (b) on all of those goods that were

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph 52(1)(a) applies, released on or after
    the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the undertaking was violated,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the ninetieth day before the day on which notice
      of termination was given under paragraph 52(1)(e); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph 52(1)(b) or (c) applies, released on
    or after the day on which notice of termination was given under
    paragraph 52(1)(e).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 3
- 1994, c. 47, ss. 145, 185(E)
- 1999, c. 12, s. 2
- 2017, c. 20, s. 69
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other cases

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to section 7.1, there shall be levied, collected and paid a duty as
  set out in subsections (3) and (4) on all dumped and subsidized goods
  imported into Canada

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which the Tribunal has made an order
    or finding, after the release of the goods, that the dumping or
    subsidizing of goods of the same description

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has caused injury, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} would have caused injury except for the fact
      that provisional duty was applied in respect of the goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that were released during the period beginning on
    the day on which the preliminary determination is made with respect
    to the goods and ending on the day on which the Tribunal makes the
    order or finding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where undertaking subsequently
  terminated

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} There shall be levied, collected and paid a duty as
  set out in subsections (3) and (4) on all dumped and subsidized goods
  imported into Canada

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that are the subject of an undertaking accepted by
    the President under subsection 49(1) that was terminated under
    paragraph 52(1)(d);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which the Tribunal has made an order
    or finding, after the release of the goods, that the dumping or
    subsidizing of goods of the same description

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has caused injury, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} would have caused injury except for the fact
      that provisional duty was applied in respect of the goods; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that were released, where paragraph 52(1)(a), (b)
    or (c) applies, during the period beginning on the day on which the
    preliminary determination was made and ending on the day the
    undertaking was accepted, and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where paragraph 52(1)(a) applies, during the
      period beginning on the later of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the undertaking is violated,
        and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the ninetieth day before the day on which
        notice of the termination was given under paragraph 52(1)(e),

      and ending on the day on which the Tribunal makes the order or
      finding referred to in paragraph (b), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where paragraph 52(1)(b) or (c) applies,
      beginning on the day on which notice of termination was given
      under paragraph 52(1)(e) and ending on the day on which the
      Tribunal makes the order or finding referred to in paragraph (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of duty

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The duty applicable to goods under subsection (1)
  or (2) is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of dumped goods, an anti-dumping duty
    in an amount that is equal to the margin of dumping of the goods;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of subsidized goods, a countervailing
    duty in an amount that is equal to the amount of subsidy on the
    goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The duty referred to in subsection (3) shall not
  exceed the duty, if any, paid or payable in respect of the goods under
  section 8.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 4
- 1988, c. 65, s. 25
- 1994, c. 47, s. 146
- 1999, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Anti-dumping duty

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** There shall be levied,
collected and paid on all dumped goods imported into Canada

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which the Tribunal has made an order or
  finding, after the release of the goods, that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} either

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} there has occurred a considerable importation of
      like goods that were dumped, which dumping has caused injury or
      would have caused injury except for the application of
      anti-dumping measures, or

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the importer of the goods was or should have been
      aware that the exporter was practising dumping and that the
      dumping would cause injury, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} injury has been caused by a massive importation of
    the goods into Canada and the goods are likely to seriously
    undermine the remedial effect of the duties applicable under
    subsection 3(1); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that were released during the period of ninety days
  preceding the day on which the President made a preliminary
  determination of dumping in respect of the goods or goods of that
  description, other than goods that were released before the initiation
  of an investigation referred to in section 31,

an anti-dumping duty in an amount equal to the margin of dumping of the
imported goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 5
- 1994, c. 47, s. 146
- 1999, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
- [2022, c. 10, s. 193](#1357060-1367840){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1357060-1367840.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20051212.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Countervailing duty

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** Where any subsidy on
subsidized goods is a prohibited subsidy, there shall be levied,
collected and paid on all such subsidized goods imported into Canada

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which the Tribunal has made an order or
  finding, after the release of the goods, that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} injury has been caused by a massive importation of
    the goods into Canada, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the goods are likely to seriously undermine the
    remedial effect of the duties applicable under subsection 3(1),

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that were released during the period of ninety days
  preceding the day on which the President made a preliminary
  determination of subsidizing in respect of the goods or goods of that
  description, other than goods that were released before the initiation
  of an investigation referred to in section 31, and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which the President has made a
  specification under clause 41(1)(b)(ii)(C),

a countervailing duty in an amount equal to such of the amount of
subsidy on the imported goods as is a prohibited subsidy.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 6
- 1994, c. 47, s. 146
- 1999, c. 12, s. 52(E), c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
- 2017, c. 20, s. 70
- [2022, c. 10, s. 194](#1357062-1367843){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1357062-1367843.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20170622.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Governor in Council may impose
countervailing duty by order

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may order an investigation to determine the amount
  of subsidy on any subsidized goods that are the product of a country
  specified in the order, and where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the President has, by means of the investigation,
    determined that amount, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Committee has authorized Canada to impose
    countervailing duties on the subsidized goods,

  the Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister of
  Finance, by order impose a countervailing duty on any subsidized goods
  that are the product of that country and that are of the same
  description as the goods in respect of which the President has
  determined the amount of subsidy and, where a countervailing duty is
  so imposed, there shall, subject to subsection (2), be levied,
  collected and paid on all such subsidized goods imported into Canada a
  countervailing duty in the amount specified in the order in respect of
  the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty not to exceed amount of subsidy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where subsidized goods on which a countervailing duty
  has been imposed pursuant to subsection (1) are imported into Canada
  and the amount of subsidy on the imported goods is less than the
  amount of the duty so imposed, there shall be levied, collected and
  paid on the goods pursuant to this section a countervailing duty only
  in the amount of subsidy on the goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 7
- 1994, c. 47, ss. 147, 185
- 1999, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

**[[7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.1 .sectionLabel}** Sections 3 and 4 do not
apply in respect of goods in respect of which an undertaking has been
accepted and not terminated.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1994, c. 47, s. 148
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of duty

**[[7.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.2 .sectionLabel}** If the Tribunal makes
an order under paragraph 76.03(12)(a), the amount that was paid as
anti-dumping or countervailing duties by or on an importer's behalf
shall be returned to the importer, in respect of goods that were
released, five years after

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the original order or finding was
  made under subsection 43(1), if no order continuing the order or
  finding that applies to those goods has been made under paragraph
  76.03(12)(b); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the last order was made, if one or
  more orders continuing the order or finding have been made under
  paragraph 76.03(12)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 7, s. 193
:::

#### [Provisional Duty]{.HTitleText3} {#h-437572 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of provisional duty

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (1.3), if the President makes a preliminary
  determination of dumping or subsidizing in an investigation under this
  Act and considers that the imposition of provisional duty is necessary
  to prevent injury, retardation or threat of injury, the importer in
  Canada of dumped or subsidized goods that are of the same description
  as any goods to which the preliminary determination applies and that
  are released during the period beginning on the day on which the
  preliminary determination is made and ending on the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the President causes the
    investigation to be terminated pursuant to subsection 41(1) with
    respect to goods of that description, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the Tribunal makes an order or
    finding with respect to goods of that description,

  shall, within the time prescribed under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for the payment of duties, at the option of the
  importer,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} pay or cause to be paid on the imported goods
    provisional duty in an amount not greater than the estimated margin
    of dumping of, or the estimated amount of subsidy on, the imported
    goods, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} post or cause to be posted security for provisional
    duty in the prescribed form and in an amount or to a value not
    greater than the estimated margin of dumping of, or the estimated
    amount of subsidy on, the imported goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of provisional duties on
  referral back to Tribunal

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (1.3), if an order or finding
  of the Tribunal under subsection 43(1), 76.02(4) respecting a review
  under subsection 76.02(1), or 91(3), other than an order or finding
  described in any of sections 3 to 6, is referred back to the Tribunal
  under subsection 77.015(3) or (4) or 77.019(5), or under subsection
  77.15(3) or (4) or 77.19(4), the importer of dumped or subsidized
  goods that are of the same description as any goods to which the order
  or finding applies and that are released during the period beginning
  on the day on which the preliminary determination is made under
  subsection 38(1) and ending on the day on which the Tribunal makes an
  order or finding, on the referral back, with respect to goods of that
  description, shall, within the time prescribed under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for the payment of duties, at the option of the
  importer,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} pay or cause to be paid on the imported goods
    provisional duty in an amount not greater than the estimated margin
    of dumping of, or the estimated amount of subsidy on, the imported
    goods; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} post or cause to be posted security for provisional
    duty in the prescribed form and in an amount or to a value not
    greater than the estimated margin of dumping of, or the estimated
    amount of subsidy on, the imported goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of provisional duties on
  referral back from Federal Court of Appeal

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (1.3), if an order or finding
  of the Tribunal under subsection 43(1), 76.02(4) respecting a review
  under subsection 76.02(1), or 91(3), other than an order or finding
  described in any of sections 3 to 6, is referred back to the Tribunal
  by the Federal Court of Appeal, the importer of dumped or subsidized
  goods that are of the same description as any goods to which the order
  or finding applies and that are released during the period beginning
  on the day on which the preliminary determination is made under
  subsection 38(1) and ending on the day on which the Tribunal makes an
  order or finding, on the referral back, with respect to goods of that
  description, shall, within the time prescribed under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for the payment of duties, at the option of the
  importer,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} pay or cause to be paid on the imported goods
    provisional duty in an amount not greater than the estimated margin
    of dumping of, or the estimated amount of subsidy on, the imported
    goods; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} post or cause to be posted security for provisional
    duty in the prescribed form and in an amount or to a value not
    greater than the estimated margin of dumping of, or the estimated
    amount of subsidy on, the imported goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insignificant margin or amount

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1), (1.1) and (1.2) do not apply in
  respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} goods of the same description as the goods
    specified in a preliminary determination in which the President
    determines that the margin of dumping of the goods is insignificant;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} goods of the same description as the goods
    specified in a preliminary determination in which the President
    determines that the amount of subsidy on the goods is insignificant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of provisional duty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any provisional duty paid or security posted under
  subsection (1), (1.1) or (1.2) by or on behalf of an importer in
  respect of the importation of dumped or subsidized goods of any
  description shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be returned to the importer forthwith after

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the President causes the investigation to be
      terminated pursuant to subsection 35.1(1) or 41(1) with respect to
      goods of that description,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all proceedings respecting the dumping or
      subsidizing of goods of that description are terminated as
      described in section 47, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the Tribunal makes an order or finding with
      respect to goods of that description if the order or finding is
      only to the effect that the dumping or subsidizing of those goods
      is threatening to cause injury; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} except to the extent of any duty payable in respect
    of the imported goods, be returned to the importer forthwith after a
    determination is made in respect of the imported goods by a
    designated officer pursuant to such of paragraphs 55(1)(c) to (e) as
    are applicable.

- [(3) and (4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 25, s. 92\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of collection

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where the President accepts an undertaking with
  respect to dumped or subsidized goods, the collection of provisional
  duties on any dumped or subsidized goods, as the case may be, that are
  of the same description as any goods to which the preliminary
  determination applies is suspended for the period during which the
  undertaking is in force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resumption of collection

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the President terminates an undertaking under
  subsection 51(1) or 52(1) with respect to dumped or subsidized goods,
  the collection of provisional duties on those goods is resumed and the
  importer of dumped or subsidized goods that are of the same
  description as any goods to which the preliminary determination under
  subsection 38(1) applied and that are released during the period
  beginning on the day on which the undertaking was terminated and
  ending on the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the President causes the
    investigation to be terminated pursuant to subsection 41(1) with
    respect to goods of that description, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the Tribunal makes an order or
    finding with respect to goods of that description,

  shall, within the time prescribed under the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) for the payment of duties, at the option of the
  importer,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} pay or cause to be paid on the imported goods
    provisional duty in an amount not greater than the estimated margin
    of dumping of, or the estimated amount of subsidy on, the imported
    goods, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} post or cause to be posted security for provisional
    duty in the prescribed form and in an amount or to a value not
    greater than the estimated margin of dumping of, or the estimated
    amount of subsidy on, the imported goods.

::: HistoricalNote

- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 198

#### [Payment of Duty During Court Proceedings and Proceedings under Part I.1 or II]{.HTitleText3} {#h-437612 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty ceases where order or finding set
aside by court

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  proceedings are commenced by an application for judicial review under
  the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7), or an application under
  section 96.1 of this Act, to review and set aside an order or finding
  of the Tribunal pursuant to which duty is leviable, collectable and
  payable (in this section referred to as "payable") under this Act on
  goods imported into Canada that are of the same description as any
  goods to which the order or finding applies, duty continues,
  notwithstanding any order or decision that may be made or given in the
  course of the proceedings, to be so payable pursuant to the order or
  finding on imported goods of that description during the course of the
  proceedings and after they are finally disposed of, unless the final
  disposition of the proceedings results in the order or finding being
  set aside or being set aside in relation to particular goods, in which
  case

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} duty ceases, on the final disposition of the
    proceedings, to be so payable on imported goods of that description
    or the same description as those particular goods, as the case may
    be; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for greater certainty, the order or finding shall,
    for the purposes of this Act, be deemed never to have been made by
    the Tribunal in respect of goods described in paragraph (a).

- Definition of [[proceedings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (1),
  [[proceedings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an application
  made to the Federal Court of Appeal, includes proceedings on any
  appeal from any decision of that Court on the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 9
- 1988, c. 65, s. 27
- 1990, c. 8, s. 69
- 2002, c. 8, s. 182
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty ceases where order or finding
rescinded pursuant to review

- **[[9.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a review is requested under
  Part I.1 of an order or finding of the Tribunal pursuant to which duty
  is leviable, collectable and payable (in this section referred to as
  "payable") under this Act on goods of a CUSMA country imported into
  Canada that are of the same description as any goods to which the
  order or finding applies, duty continues, notwithstanding any order or
  decision that may be made or given in the course of proceedings under
  that Part, to be so payable pursuant to the order or finding on
  imported goods of that description during the course of the
  proceedings and after they are finally disposed of, unless the final
  disposition of the proceedings results in the order or finding being
  rescinded or being rescinded in relation to particular goods, in which
  case

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} duty ceases, on the order or finding being so
    rescinded, to be so payable on imported goods of that description or
    the same description as those particular goods, as the case may be;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for greater certainty, the order or finding shall,
    for the purposes of this Act, be deemed never to have been made by
    the Tribunal in respect of goods described in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of s. 9.1

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operation of section 9.1 is suspended during the
  period in which subsection (1) is in force.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 204
- [2020, c. 1, s. 73](#1237750-1253717){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1237750-1253717.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-9.01-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 9.01"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty ceases where order or finding
rescinded pursuant to review

**[[9.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.1 .sectionLabel}** Where a review is
requested under Part II of an order or finding of the Tribunal pursuant
to which duty is leviable, collectable and payable (in this section
referred to as "payable") under this Act on goods of the United States
imported into Canada that are of the same description as any goods to
which the order or finding applies, duty continues, notwithstanding any
order or decision that may be made or given in the course of proceedings
under that Part, to be so payable pursuant to the order or finding on
imported goods of that description during the course of the proceedings
and after they are finally disposed of, unless the final disposition of
the proceedings results in the order or finding being rescinded or being
rescinded in relation to particular goods, in which case

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} duty ceases, on the order or finding being so
  rescinded, to be so payable on imported goods of that description or
  the same description as those particular goods, as the case may be;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for greater certainty, the order or finding shall,
  for the purposes of this Act, be deemed never to have been made by the
  Tribunal in respect of goods described in paragraph (a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1988, c. 65, s. 28
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty ceases if final determination set
aside by court

- **[[9.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  duty is leviable, collectable and payable (in this section referred to
  as "payable") under this Act under an order or finding of the Tribunal
  on goods imported into Canada, and proceedings are commenced in the
  Federal Court of Appeal by an application under section 96.1 to review
  and set aside the final determination of the President under paragraph
  41(1)(b) on which the order or finding is based, duty continues,
  despite any order or decision that may be made or given in the course
  of the proceedings, to be so payable under the order or finding on
  imported goods of the same description as those goods during the
  course of the proceedings and after they are finally disposed of,
  unless the final disposition of the proceedings results in the final
  determination being set aside or being set aside in relation to
  particular goods, or the President recommencing the investigation and
  terminating it under paragraph 41(1)(a), in which case

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} duty ceases, on the final determination being so
    set aside or the investigation being so terminated, to be so payable
    on imported goods of that description or the same description as
    those particular goods, as the case may be; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for greater certainty, the order or finding shall,
    for the purposes of this Act, be deemed never to have been made by
    the Tribunal in respect of goods described in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[proceedings]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (1),
  [[proceedings]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, in relation to an application
  made to the Federal Court of Appeal, includes proceedings on any
  appeal from any decision of that Court on the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1988, c. 65, s. 28
- 1999, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
- 2017, c. 20, s. 71
:::

Version](section-9.2-20051212.html "Link to previous version of section 9.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty ceases if investigation terminated
after review

- **[[9.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  duty is leviable, collectable and payable (in this section referred to
  as "payable") under this Act under an order or finding of the Tribunal
  on goods of a CUSMA country imported into Canada, and a review is
  requested under Part I.1 of the final determination of the President
  under paragraph 41(1)(b) on which the order or finding is based, duty
  continues, despite any order or decision that may be made or given in
  the course of proceedings under that Part, to be so payable under the
  order or finding on imported goods of the same description as those
  goods during the course of the proceedings and after they are finally
  disposed of, unless the final disposition of the proceedings results
  in the President recommencing the investigation and terminating it
  under paragraph 41(1)(a), in which case

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} duty ceases, on the investigation being so
    terminated, to be so payable on imported goods of that description;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for greater certainty, the order or finding shall,
    for the purposes of this Act, be deemed never to have been made by
    the Tribunal in respect of imported goods of that description.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of s. 9.3

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operation of section 9.3 is suspended during the
  period in which subsection (1) is in force.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty ceases if investigation terminated
after review

**[[9.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.3 .sectionLabel}** If duty is leviable,
collectable and payable (in this section referred to as "payable") under
this Act under an order or finding of the Tribunal on goods of the
United States imported into Canada, and a review is requested under Part
II of the final determination of the President under paragraph 41(1)(b)
on which the order or finding is based, duty continues, despite any
order or decision that may be made or given in the course of proceedings
under that Part, to be so payable under the order or finding on imported
goods of the same description as those goods during the course of the
proceedings and after they are finally disposed of, unless the final
disposition of the proceedings results in the President recommencing the
investigation and terminating it under paragraph 41(1)(a), in which case

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} duty ceases, on the investigation being so
  terminated, to be so payable on imported goods of that description;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for greater certainty, the order or finding shall,
  for the purposes of this Act, be deemed never to have been made by the
  Tribunal in respect of imported goods of that description.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1988, c. 65, s. 28
- 1999, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
- 2017, c. 20, s. 73
:::
