### [Ruling on Who is Importer]{.HTitleText2} {#h-439606 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for ruling on who is importer in
Canada

- **[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a question arises or is raised as to which of two or more persons is,
  for the purposes of this Act, the importer in Canada of goods imported
  or to be imported into Canada on which duty is payable or has been
  paid or will be payable if the goods are imported, the President may,
  and at the request of any person interested in the importation of the
  goods shall, request the Tribunal for a ruling on that question,
  unless, in the case only of goods that have been imported into Canada,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a determination has been made under section 55 or
    56 with respect to the goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} more than ninety days have elapsed since the
    determination referred to in paragraph (a) was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the President makes a request under
  subsection (1) for a ruling on the question referred to therein, the
  President shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} state in the request which of the two or more
    persons the President believes is the importer in Canada of the
    goods;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if any of the goods is of the same description as
    the goods specified in a preliminary determination made in an
    investigation that was initiated pursuant to section 31 and is still
    continuing, so state in the request;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} provide the Tribunal with such information as the
    President considers will be useful to it in considering the question
    and with such other information as the Tribunal may request; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} give notice of his request to such persons as the
    rules of the Tribunal require or as the Tribunal may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investigation deemed to continue

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where, in any investigation, the President makes a
  final determination of dumping or subsidizing under subsection 41(1)
  in respect of any goods, the investigation shall, for the purpose of
  paragraph (2)(b), be deemed to continue until such time as the
  Tribunal makes an order or finding in respect of the goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 89
- 1999, c. 12, s. 46, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-89-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 89"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tribunal's ruling

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** Where a request is made
to the Tribunal under subsection 89(1) for a ruling on the question
referred to therein, the Tribunal

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall arrive at its ruling on the question by
  determining which of the two or more persons is the importer in Canada
  of the goods;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph (c), shall give its ruling on
  the question forthwith after receiving the request therefor; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} shall not, if a statement pursuant to paragraph
  89(2)(b) is made in the request, give its ruling on the question until
  after it makes an order or finding in the inquiry commenced as a
  consequence of its receipt of notice of the preliminary determination
  referred to in that paragraph, unless, after the request is made to
  the Tribunal, it receives notice pursuant to subsection 41(4) that the
  investigation has been terminated pursuant to subsection 41(1) in
  respect of the goods specified in the preliminary determination, in
  which case the Tribunal shall give its ruling on the question
  forthwith after it receives that notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 90
- 2014, c. 20, s. 441
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-90-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 90"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules

- **[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a request is made to the Tribunal pursuant to
    subsection 89(1) for a ruling on the question referred to therein,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement pursuant to paragraph 89(2)(b) is made
    in the request, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Tribunal's ruling on the question is that the
    importer in Canada of the goods is a person other than the person
    specified as such by the President pursuant to paragraph 89(2)(a),

  the following rules apply:

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} as soon as possible after the Tribunal gives its
    ruling on the question, the President shall

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} reconsider any final determination of dumping or
      subsidizing made pursuant to subsection 41(1) with respect to the
      goods specified in the preliminary determination and shall confirm
      the final determination, rescind it or make amendments to it, as
      is appropriate in the circumstances, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} cause notice of the action taken by the
      President pursuant to subparagraph (i) to be given to prescribed
      persons and governments, published in the [Canada
      Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and filed with the Tribunal in
      writing;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} where the President rescinds a final determination
    pursuant to paragraph (d), section 41 shall again apply in respect
    of the goods to which the final determination applied as if that
    section had not previously applied in respect of those goods, except
    that the action that the President is required by that section to
    take shall, notwithstanding anything therein, be taken by the
    President within sixty days after the Tribunal gives its ruling on
    the question;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} where the President has caused the investigation
    referred to in paragraph 89(2)(b) to be terminated pursuant to
    subsection 41(1) with respect to the goods specified in the
    preliminary determination, the Tribunal shall be deemed to have
    directed the President, by notice in writing pursuant to section 46,
    to cause an investigation to be initiated respecting the dumping or
    subsidizing of those goods and the President shall, pursuant to
    subsection 31(2), forthwith cause such an investigation to be
    commenced; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the Tribunal may, on its own initiative or at the
    request of the President or any person interested but subject to
    subsection (2), reconsider, under the authority of this paragraph,
    any order or finding made by it in the inquiry referred to in
    paragraph 90(c) and, in so reconsidering, may re-hear any matter
    before deciding it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on reconsideration of order
  or finding

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Tribunal shall not commence reconsideration of an
  order or finding under the authority of paragraph (1)(g)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} later than ninety days after the making of the
    ruling on the question referred to in paragraph (1)(a); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the request of any person unless that person
    satisfies the Tribunal that reconsideration of the order or finding
    is warranted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Completion of reconsideration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the Tribunal reconsiders an order or finding
  under the authority of paragraph (1)(g),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Tribunal shall complete the reconsideration
    forthwith and, in any event, not later than ninety days after the
    day on which it decides to commence it and, on completion thereof,
    shall confirm the order or finding or rescind it and make such other
    order or finding with respect to the goods to which the order or
    finding under reconsideration applies as the nature of the matter
    may require, and, where it makes another order or finding, shall
    declare to what goods, including, where applicable, from what
    supplier and from what country of export, the order or finding
    applies;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Tribunal shall forward by registered mail to
    the President, the importer, the exporter and such other persons and
    governments as may be specified by the rules of the Tribunal

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} forthwith after the reconsideration is completed,
      notice of the action taken pursuant to paragraph (a) with respect
      to the order or finding and, where another order or finding has
      been made pursuant to that paragraph, a copy of that other order
      or finding, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} not later than fifteen days after the completion
      of the reconsideration, a copy of the reasons for the action taken
      thereon; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where the Tribunal makes another order or finding
    pursuant to paragraph (a), it shall cause notice of the order or
    finding to be published in the [Canada
    Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate order or finding

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a reconsideration under the authority of
  paragraph (1)(g) involves goods of the United States as well as goods
  of other countries and the Tribunal makes another order or finding
  under paragraph (3)(a), the Tribunal shall make a separate order or
  finding under that paragraph with respect to the goods of the United
  States.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 91
- 1988, c. 65, s. 43
- 1999, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
- 2014, c. 20, ss. 442, 443
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-91-20051212.html "Link to previous version of section 91"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination pursuant to section 55

**[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** A determination made
pursuant to section 55 in respect of any imported goods on the basis
that the importer of the goods was a person who is subsequently ruled by
the Tribunal not to have been the importer thereof shall be deemed not
to have been made and, for the purpose of that section, the date of the
order or finding of the Tribunal with respect to goods that appear to be
of the same description as the imported goods shall be deemed to be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} where, following its ruling, the Tribunal reconsiders
  the order or finding pursuant to paragraph 91(1)(g) and confirms it,
  the date on which the Tribunal confirms the order or finding;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} where, following its ruling, the Tribunal reconsiders
  the order or finding pursuant to paragraph 91(1)(g) and rescinds it
  and makes another order or finding with respect to goods of that
  description, the date of the other order or finding; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the date of the Tribunal's ruling.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1984, c. 25, s. 92
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination pursuant to section 56, 57
or 59

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** A determination or
re-determination made pursuant to section 56, 57 or 59 in respect of any
goods on the basis that the importer of the goods was a person who is
subsequently ruled by the Tribunal not to have been the importer thereof
shall be deemed not to have been made and the goods shall, for the
purposes of section 56, be deemed to be accounted for on the earlier of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day that is sixty days after the day on which the
  Tribunal made the ruling; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a new determination is made pursuant
  to section 56 in respect of the goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 93
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 211
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ruling binding

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** A ruling given by the
Tribunal on the question of who is the importer in Canada of any goods
imported or to be imported into Canada is binding on the President, and
on every person employed by the Canada Border Services Agency in the
administration or enforcement of this Act, with respect to the
particular goods in relation to which the ruling is given, unless the
Tribunal is fraudulently misled or, in the case only of goods to be
imported into Canada, material facts that are not available to the
President at the time the Tribunal gives its ruling come to the
President's attention after it is given.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 94
- 1999, c. 17, s. 182
- 2005, c. 38, s. 133
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-94-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 94"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}President to provide name of importer

**[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** Where any person
interested in the importation of goods into Canada requests the
President to provide the person with the name of the importer of the
goods, the President shall, except in prescribed circumstances,
forthwith provide the person with the name of the importer.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 95
- 1999, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
:::

### [Gathering of Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-439678 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}President may gather information in
advance

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** In order to facilitate
the administration and enforcement of this Act, where the President
believes that goods sold to an importer in Canada or goods located or in
the course of production out of Canada are or may be of the same
description as goods to which an order or finding of the Tribunal
described in section 3, 5 or 6 applies and that they will or may be
imported into Canada, the President may, for the purpose of estimating
the margin of dumping of or the amount of subsidy on the goods before
they are imported into Canada, seek from persons in or out of Canada, in
such manner and form as he considers appropriate in the circumstances,
such information as he believes will be useful for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 96
- 1994, c. 47, s. 185
- 1999, c. 17, s. 183
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-96-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 96"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application for Review]{.HTitleText2} {#h-439683 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for judicial review

- **[[96.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 77.012 or 77.12,
  an application may be made to the Federal Court of Appeal to review
  and set aside

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a decision of the President under paragraph
    41(1)(a);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a final determination of the President under
    paragraph 41(1)(b);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a decision of the President under subsection 53(1)
    to renew or not to renew an undertaking;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} an order or finding of the Tribunal under
    subsection 43(1);

  - [(c.2)]{.lawlabel} a decision of the President under subsection
    75.1(1);

  - [(c.3)]{.lawlabel} a decision of the President under subsection
    75.4(6);

  - [(c.4)]{.lawlabel} a determination of the President under subsection
    75.6(5);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an order of the Tribunal under subsection 76.01(4);

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} a determination of the President under paragraph
    76.03(7)(a);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an order or finding of the Tribunal under
    subsection 76.02(4) respecting a review under subsection 76.02(1);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an order of the Tribunal under subsection 76.01(5)
    or 76.03(12); or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an order or finding of the Tribunal under
    subsection 91(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application may be made under this section on the
  ground that the President or the Tribunal, as the case may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acted without jurisdiction, acted beyond the
    jurisdiction of the President or the Tribunal or refused to exercise
    that jurisdiction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} failed to observe a principle of natural justice,
    procedural fairness or other procedure that the President or the
    Tribunal was required by law to observe;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} erred in law in making a decision or an order,
    whether or not the error appears on the face of the record;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} based a decision or order on an erroneous finding
    of fact that the President or the Tribunal made in a perverse or
    capricious manner or without regard for the material before the
    President or the Tribunal;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} acted, or failed to act, by reason of fraud or
    perjured evidence; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} acted in any other way that was contrary to law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 77.012(2), an application may
  be made under this section by any person directly affected by the
  determination, decision, order or finding by filing a notice of the
  application in the Federal Court of Appeal within thirty days after
  the time the determination, decision, order or finding was first
  communicated to that person by the President or the Tribunal, or
  within such further time as the Federal Court of Appeal or a judge
  thereof may, before or after the expiration of those thirty days, fix
  or allow.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trial Division deprived of jurisdiction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the Federal Court of Appeal has jurisdiction
  under this section to hear and determine an application to review and
  set aside a determination, decision, order or finding, the Trial
  Division has no jurisdiction to entertain any proceeding in respect of
  that determination, decision, order or finding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hearing in summary way

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An application under this section shall be heard and
  determined without delay and in a summary way in accordance with the
  rules made in respect of applications for judicial review pursuant to
  sections 18.1 and 28 of the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On an application under this section, the Federal
  Court of Appeal may dismiss the application, set aside the final
  determination, decision, order or finding, or set aside the final
  determination, decision, order or finding and refer the matter back to
  the President or the Tribunal, as the case may be, for determination
  in accordance with such directions as it considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No references

- **[[96.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 18.3(1) of the [Federal
  Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7) does not apply to the President or the
  Tribunal in respect of proceedings under this Act relating to goods of
  a CUSMA country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of s. 96.2

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operation of section 96.2 is suspended during the
  period in which subsection (1) is in force.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 221

the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7) does not apply to the President
or the Tribunal in respect of proceedings under this Act relating to
goods of the United States.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review of final determination

- **[[96.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister for International Trade
  may, in the manner provided for by the law of a CUSMA country giving
  effect to the Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement, request that a
  final determination be reviewed by a panel established under that law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person who, but for the law of a CUSMA country
  giving effect to the Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement, would be
  entitled under the law of that CUSMA country to commence domestic
  proceedings for judicial review of a final determination may file with
  the Canadian Secretary a request that the final determination be
  reviewed by a panel established under that law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deeming

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A request under subsection (2) shall be deemed to be
  a request by the Minister for binational review within the meaning of
  paragraph 4 of Article 10.12 of the Canada--United States--Mexico
  Agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A request under subsection (1) or (2) may only be
  made within 30 days after the day on which notice of the final
  determination is published in the official publication of the CUSMA
  country, or, in the case of a final determination of which notice is
  not so published, within 30 days after the day on which notice of the
  final determination is received by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[final
  determination]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[final
  determination]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a [[final
  determination]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in Article 10.8 of
  the Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of s. 96.3

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The operation of section 96.3 is suspended during the
  period in which this section is in force.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 222
- 1999, c. 12, s. 48(F)
- [2020, c. 1, s. 103](#1237947-1253878){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1237947-1253878.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-96.21-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 96.21"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review of final determination

- **[[96.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister for International Trade
  may, in the manner provided for by the American law giving effect to
  the Free Trade Agreement, request that a final determination be
  reviewed by a panel established under that law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On a request made to the Canadian Secretary by any
  person who, but for the American law giving effect to the Free Trade
  Agreement, would be entitled under American law to commence domestic
  proceedings for judicial review of a final determination, the Minister
  for International Trade shall, in the manner provided for by the
  American law giving effect to the Free Trade Agreement, request that
  the final determination be reviewed by a panel established under that
  law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No request shall be made to the Canadian Secretary
  under subsection (2) more than twenty-five days after the day on which
  notice of the final determination is published in the Federal Register
  or, in the case of a final determination of which notice is not so
  published, the day on which notice of the final determination is
  received by the Minister.

- Definition of [[final determination]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[final
  determination]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a final determination as
  defined in subparagraph (b) of the definition of that term in Article
  1911 of the Free Trade Agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1988, c. 65, s. 44
- 1999, c. 12, s. 49(F)
:::

### [Offences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-439754 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

- **[[96.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person commits an offence who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} uses information disclosed to the person by the
    President under subsection 84(3) for any purpose other than the
    purpose for which the information was disclosed under that
    subsection; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contravenes a condition imposed by the President
    under subsection 84(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under
  subsection (1) is guilty of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indictable offence and liable to a fine of not
    more than \$1,000,000; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an offence punishable on summary conviction and
    liable to a fine of not more than \$100,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No proceedings for an offence under this section
  shall be instituted without the consent in writing of the Attorney
  General of Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 12, s. 50, c. 17, s. 184
- 2005, c. 38, s. 134
:::

## [Regulations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-439768 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister of
  Finance, make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that by this Act is to be or
    may be prescribed by regulation;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} respecting the factors that may be considered in
    determining

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the existence of injury, retardation or threat of
      injury,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} whether the injury, retardation or threat of
      injury has been caused by the dumping or subsidizing of any goods
      or by any other reason,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} whether there has been a change in the pattern
      of trade,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} whether the process of assembly or completion is
      insignificant,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} whether a change in a pattern of trade is caused
      by the imposition of an anti-dumping or countervailing duty, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} whether a prescribed activity is undermining the
      remedial effects of an order of the Governor in Council or an
      order or finding;

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} respecting activities for the purposes of
    paragraph 71(b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifying the circumstances and manner in which
    two or more properly documented complaints, investigations or
    inquiries, including anti-circumvention complaints and
    investigations and scope ruling applications and scope proceedings,
    may be joined and carried on as one and the persons to whom and the
    manner in which notice of the joining shall be given;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purpose of subsection 74(2),
    what constitutes a sufficient amount of time for interested parties
    to provide written comments;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} defining the expression [[duty or internal
    tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purpose of the definition
    [[subsidy]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} defining the expressions [[cost of
    production]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[a reasonable amount for
    administrative, selling and all other costs]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
    and [[a reasonable amount for profits]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for
    the purpose of paragraph 19(b) or subparagraph 20(1)(c)(ii);

  - [(e.1)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner of calculating the cost of
    production of goods and the administrative, selling and all other
    costs with respect to goods;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} defining the expression [[an amount for
    profit]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purpose of subparagraph
    25(1)(c)(ii) or (d)(i);

  - [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} defining the expression [[start-up period of
    production]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purposes of section 23.1,
    including prescribing the factors to consider in determining the
    duration of such a period;

  - [(f.2)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of subsection
    30.3(3), the manner for determining a margin of dumping, including
    prescribing the manner for determining the maximum margin of dumping
    that can be determined;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} defining the expression [[person
    interested]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purpose of subsection
    45(6) or section 89 or 95 and the expression [[interested
    person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purpose of subsections
    61(1.1), 63(1) or 67(4);

  - [(g.01)]{.lawlabel} prescribing what constitutes a complete
    application for the purposes of subsection 63(7);

  - [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} deeming a government in Canada or the United
    States to be a person who is entitled to make a request to the
    Canadian Secretary under subsection 77.11(2);

  - [(g.11)]{.lawlabel} deeming a government in Canada or in a CUSMA
    country to be a person who is entitled to make a request to the
    Canadian Secretary under subsection 77.011(2);

  - [(g.2)]{.lawlabel} defining the expression [[goods of the United
    States]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purpose of this Act;

  - [(g.21)]{.lawlabel} defining the expression [[goods of a CUSMA
    country]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purpose of this Act;

  - [(g.22)]{.lawlabel} determining, in respect of each CUSMA country,
    which publication shall be deemed to be the official publication of
    that country for the purpose of this Act;

  - [(g.23)]{.lawlabel} determining the meaning of the expression
    [[goods of Chile]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purposes of this
    Act;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the procedure to be followed in an
    investigation ordered by the Governor in Council under subsection
    7(1);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} providing for the selection of an interest rate,
    from among or by reference to interest rates prevailing in or out of
    Canada at the time of the sale referred to in subsection 21(1), by
    reference to which the determination referred to in paragraph
    21(1)(a) shall be made in the circumstances described in clause
    21(1)(a)(ii)(B);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} providing for the selection of an interest rate,
    from among or by reference to interest rates prevailing in or out of
    Canada at the time of the sale referred to in subsection 27(1), by
    reference to which the determination referred to in paragraph
    27(1)(a) shall be made in the circumstances described in clause
    27(1)(a)(ii)(B);

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} providing for the determination of or specifying
    the date as of which the equivalent dollar value of any amount that
    is expressed in the currency of a country other than Canada and that
    is used or taken into account for any purpose in the administration
    or enforcement of this Act shall be ascertained, determined or
    calculated;

  - [(k.1)]{.lawlabel} providing for the method of determining the rate
    of exchange for the purpose of calculating the export price for
    export sales involving the sale of foreign currency on forward
    markets;

  - [(k.2)]{.lawlabel} providing for the manner of making adjustments to
    export prices and normal values in situations of fluctuation or
    sustained movement in the rate of exchange;

  - [(k.3)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the period after which the President
    may refuse to consider representations referred to in subsection
    49(5);

  - [(k.4)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the factors that the President may
    consider in making a determination under paragraph 76.03(7)(a);

  - [(k.5)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the factors that the Tribunal may
    consider in making a determination under subsection 76.03(10);

  - [(k.6)]{.lawlabel} providing for the manner of attributing principal
    and interest to imported goods when those amounts include a portion
    related to charges not directly associated with the value of the
    goods; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} generally, for carrying out the purposes and
    provisions of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations prescribing rate of
  interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister of Finance, make regulations prescribing a rate of
  interest or rules for determining a rate of interest for the purposes
  of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. S-15, s. 97
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 212

## [Orders]{.HTitleText1} {#h-439812 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Orders suspending application

- **[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, for the purpose of ensuring that this Act
  complies with the Subsidies Agreement, by order, modify or suspend the
  application of any provision, in whole or in part, of this Act with
  respect to any country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless revoked, an order made under subsection (1)
  has effect for the period specified in the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2000, c. 14, s. 47
:::

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2002, c. 8, s. 192

  - ###### [Special Import Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) {#special-import-measures-act-1 .MarginalNote}

    [192]{.lawlabel} Subsection 12(1.1), paragraphs 44(2)(a) and
    59(1)(d) and subsections 77.01(1) and 77.1(1) of the [Special Import
    Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15), as enacted or amended by sections 169
    to 173 of this Act, apply to goods from a NAFTA country, as defined
    in subsection 2(1) of that Act.

- --- 2005, c. 38, s. 137

  - ###### Application {#application .MarginalNote}

    [137]{.lawlabel} The provisions of the [Special Import Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/S-15), as enacted or amended by sections 132 to 136
    and paragraph 145(2)(i) of this Act, apply to goods of a NAFTA
    country, as defined in subsection 2(1) of that Act.

- --- 2014, c. 20, s. 444

  - ###### Application {#application-1 .MarginalNote}

    [444]{.lawlabel} The following provisions of the Act, as enacted or
    amended by sections 429 to 443, apply to goods of a NAFTA country,
    as defined in subsection 2(1) of the Act:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 34(1)(a)(i) and subsection 34(2);

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 35(2)(b);

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 38(3)(b);

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} paragraphs 41(3)(b) and (4)(b);

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsections 41.1(1) and (2);

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsections 42(1) and (2);

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} subsections 43(1) to (3);

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} subsection 44(2);

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 45(2);

    - [(j)]{.lawlabel} subsection 47(3);

    - [(k)]{.lawlabel} paragraphs 52(1)(e) and (1.1)(e);

    - [(l)]{.lawlabel} subsection 53(4);

    - [(m)]{.lawlabel} subsections 53.1(1) and (2);

    - [(n)]{.lawlabel} subsections 61(1) and (2);

    - [(o)]{.lawlabel} subsections 76.01(4) and (6);

    - [(p)]{.lawlabel} subsection 76.02(5);

    - [(q)]{.lawlabel} subsections 76.03(2), (5) and (6) and paragraph
      76.03(7)(b);

    - [(r)]{.lawlabel} section 77.14;

    - [(s)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 90(c); and

    - [(t)]{.lawlabel} subparagraph 91(1)(d)(ii) and paragraphs 91(3)(b)
      and (c).

- --- 2016, c. 7, s. 200

  - ###### Application {#application-2 .MarginalNote}

    [200]{.lawlabel} The provisions of the [Special Import Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/S-15), as enacted or amended by sections 192 to 199,
    apply to goods from a [[NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in subsection 2(1) of that Act.

- --- 2017, c. 20, s. 99

  - ###### Definitions {#definitions .MarginalNote}

    [99]{.lawlabel} The definitions in this section apply in this
    section and sections 100 and 101.

    [[commencement day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[commencement day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day on which
        this section comes into force. ([date de
        référence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Special Import
        Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) as it read on the day before the
        commencement day. ([ancienne loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[new Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[new Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Special Import
        Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) as it read on the commencement
        day. ([nouvelle loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- --- 2017, c. 20, s. 100

  - ###### Disposition of notified complaints {#disposition-of-notified-complaints .MarginalNote}

    - [100]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to
      (7), if, before the commencement day, notice of a complaint
      respecting the dumping or subsidizing of goods that is [[properly
      documented]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1)
      of the former Act, has been given under paragraph 32(1)(a) of that
      Act, any proceeding, process or action in respect of the goods
      shall be continued and disposed of in accordance with that Act.

    - ###### Goods subject to order made after commencement day {#goods-subject-to-order-made-after-commencement-day .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Canadian International Trade Tribunal
      makes an order or finding under subsection 43(1) of the new Act on
      or after the commencement day with respect to goods that are the
      subject of a complaint referred to in subsection (1), any
      subsequent proceeding, process or action in respect of those goods
      other than the following shall be disposed of in accordance with
      that Act:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a judicial review or dispute settlement under
        Part I.1 or II of the new Act in relation to that order or
        finding and any proceeding, process or action in relation to the
        judicial review or dispute settlement;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a proceeding, process or action in relation to
        any of those goods that were released before the commencement
        day;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a proceeding, process or action in relation to
        any of those goods that were released on or after the
        commencement day but on or before the day on which the Tribunal
        made the order or finding; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a proceeding, process or action under section
        45 of the new Act in relation to that order or finding.

    - ###### Effect of order or finding {#effect-of-order-or-finding .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, any order or finding that
      was made before the commencement day and is in effect on that day
      shall, for the purposes of sections 3 to 6 of the new Act, have
      the same force and effect as if it were made under that Act.

    - ###### New Act does not justify review {#new-act-does-not-justify-review .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection 76.01(3) of the new
      Act, the fact that this Act comes into force is not sufficient
      reason for the Canadian International Trade Tribunal to be
      satisfied that an interim review of an order or finding is
      warranted.

    - ###### Determination --- undertaking {#determination-undertaking .MarginalNote}

      [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any determination, on or after the commencement
      day, of a normal value, export price, amount of subsidy or margin
      of dumping in relation to any goods that are subject to an
      undertaking accepted before the commencement day shall be made in
      accordance with the new Act.

    - ###### Determination --- deeming {#determination-deeming .MarginalNote}

      [(6)]{.lawlabel} A determination of a normal value, export price,
      amount of subsidy or margin of dumping made in relation to goods
      under the former Act is, for the purposes of goods released on or
      after the commencement day, other than goods to which paragraph
      (2)(c) applies, deemed to have been made under the new Act.

    - ###### Re-determination of normal value, etc. {#re-determination-of-normal-value-etc. .MarginalNote}

      [(7)]{.lawlabel} A re-determination of a normal value, export
      price, amount of subsidy or margin of dumping referred to in
      subsection (6) shall be made in accordance with the new Act.

- --- 2017, c. 20, s. 101

  - ###### Application {#application-3 .MarginalNote}

    [101]{.lawlabel} The provisions of the new Act, as enacted or
    amended by sections 68 to 98, apply to goods from a [[NAFTA
    country]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1) of
    that Act.

- --- 2020, c. 1, s. 105

  - ###### Definition of [[commencement day]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} {#definition-of-commencement-day .MarginalNote}

    [105]{.lawlabel} In sections 106 and 107, [[commencement
    day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day fixed under subsection
    213(1).

- --- 2020, c. 1, s. 106

  - ###### Pending proceedings {#pending-proceedings .MarginalNote}

    [106]{.lawlabel} Proceedings that are pending under the [Special
    Import Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) immediately before the
    commencement day and that are in relation to goods of a [[NAFTA
    country]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as that expression is defined in
    subsection 2(1) of that Act as it read immediately before that day,
    are continued under that Act as it reads on that day and the
    proceedings are deemed to be in relation to goods of a [[CUSMA
    country]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as that expression is defined in
    subsection 2(1) of that Act as it reads on that day.

- --- 2020, c. 1, s. 107

  - ###### New proceedings {#new-proceedings .MarginalNote}

    [107]{.lawlabel} If proceedings under the [Special Import Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/S-15) are commenced on or after the commencement day
    in respect of goods that were imported before the commencement day
    and that were, on the day on which they were imported, goods of a
    [[NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as that expression is
    defined in subsection 2(1) of that Act as it read immediately before
    the commencement day, the proceedings are deemed to be in relation
    to goods of a [[CUSMA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as that
    expression is defined in subsection 2(1) of that Act as it reads on
    the commencement day.

- --- 2022, c. 10, s. 207

  - ###### Definitions {#definitions-1 .MarginalNote}

    - [207]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply
      in this section and sections 208 to 211.

      [[commencement day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[commencement day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day on
          which this Act receives royal assent. ([date de
          référence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

      [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Special Import
          Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) as it read on the day before the
          commencement day. ([ancienne loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    - ###### Words and expressions {#words-and-expressions .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Words and expressions used in sections 208 to 211
      have the same meaning as in the [Special Import Measures
      Act](/eng/acts/S-15).

- --- 2022, c. 10, s. 208

  - ###### Disposition of complaints {#disposition-of-complaints .MarginalNote}

    [208]{.lawlabel} If, before the commencement day, the President
    receives a written complaint respecting the dumping or subsidizing
    of goods under subsection 31(1) of the former Act, any proceeding,
    process or action in respect of the complaint is to be continued and
    disposed of in accordance with that Act.

- --- 2022, c. 10, s. 209

  - ###### Anti-circumvention complaints {#anti-circumvention-complaints .MarginalNote}

    [209]{.lawlabel} If, before the commencement day, the President
    receives a written complaint under subsection 72(1) of the former
    Act respecting the circumvention of an order or finding of the
    Tribunal, or an order of the Governor in Council imposing a
    countervailing duty under section 7 of that Act, any proceeding,
    process or action in respect of the complaint is to be continued and
    disposed of in accordance with that Act.

- --- 2022, c. 10, s. 210

  - ###### Interim reviews --- on request {#interim-reviews-on-request .MarginalNote}

    - [210]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, before the commencement day,
      the Tribunal receives a request to conduct an interim review of an
      order or finding, or any aspect of an order or finding, under
      subsection 76.01(1) of the former Act, any interim review is to be
      initiated --- or, if already initiated, continued --- and disposed
      of in accordance with that Act.

    - ###### Interim reviews --- Tribunal's initiative {#interim-reviews-tribunals-initiative .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, before the commencement day, the Tribunal
      initiates, on its own initiative, an interim review of an order or
      finding, or any aspect of an order or finding, under subsection
      76.01(1) of the former Act, the interim review is to be continued
      and disposed of in accordance with that Act.

- --- 2022, c. 10, s. 211

  - ###### Expiry reviews {#expiry-reviews .MarginalNote}

    [211]{.lawlabel} If, before the commencement day, a notice of expiry
    respecting an order or finding has been published under subsection
    76.03(2) of the former Act, any expiry review in respect of the
    order or finding is to be initiated --- or, if already initiated,
    continued --- and disposed of in accordance with that Act.
